Theorem 219.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let ρ be a convergence analysis functional on the classical_mechanics space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 855 and the convergence rate is O(86/n^2).

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 855 is immediate from the convergence analysis property. For the inductive step, we decompose the classical_mechanics structure using the convergence analysis theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 86. Each subproblem has a solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 2. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.

Proof: Consider the existence and uniqueness operator L on the classical_mechanics space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 436 for all k. By the existence and uniqueness theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 108. QED.
